Thus girth and linearity hold. The witness table and all costs are polynomial for each fixed $g$. This is not a uniform construction for growing $g$ and the hub may have large degree.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[Both-label critical diagonal, conditional on separation]\label{lem:do16-diag}
Fix a total exact deterministic solver on the certified domain of Lemma~\ref{lem:do16-critical}, or of Lemma~\ref{lem:do16-girth} for any fixed $g$. If $\mathrm P\ne\mathrm{NP}$, its runtime has no polynomial upper bound on the SAT side alone and no polynomial upper bound on the UNSAT side alone. Consequently there are strictly length-growing diagonal sequences of each label satisfying the inequality of Lemma~\ref{lem:diagonal}; on the UNSAT sequence all inputs are critical and all deletion witnesses are supplied. Each sequence eventually escapes every fixed polynomially scheduled terminal domain.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
If runtime is bounded by a fixed polynomial $p$ on the SAT side, simulate for $p$ steps and answer UNSAT on timeout. This is correct on the promised domain by exactness and the one-sided bound. If bounded on the UNSAT side, answer SAT on timeout instead. Either algorithm composed with the uniform reduction decides arbitrary 3-CNF in polynomial time. The argument does not require deciding the promise outside the reduction image. Applying Lemma~\ref{lem:diagonal} to each label separately gives the sequences, with finitely many short inputs absorbed by raising the exponent. The same result holds for any fixed exact total solver which ignores the table, measuring chart-only encoded length, since the reduction also has polynomial chart length. In particular a uniform polynomial bound on all critical UNSAT signed NAE charts alone would imply P=NP (run the timeout test on this reduction). Without the separation hypothesis, a slow fixed portfolio on arbitrary inputs need not be slow on this normal form. A diagonal family on a prescribed class of growing girth and constant degree does not follow.
\end{proof}

\paragraph{Load-bearing constructive consequence.}
This identifies an equivalent U-PROGRESS subproblem with a polynomially acquired witness per owner, not just one near-model. A uniform polynomial algorithm (including all discovery and verification) distinguishing a genuine model from a critical cover on this promised domain suffices. The deletion witnesses make every forbidden packet have a private point relative to the other packets; hence no exact deletion of a nonempty owner group can preserve UNSAT on the negative side. They do not certify that no uncovered point exists. Any procedure that merely checks all witnesses, or sees that every deletion is SAT, fails on the SAT outputs, which have exactly the same certified property. An all-owner witness table is not an aggregate contraction proof; remaining signed-basis compatibility, joint circulation repair, or another exact nonlocal decision remains necessary. Do not infer NP-hardness of the narrower four-perfect-partition acquisition step in S21, or growing-girth bounded-degree diagonal identification. Same-realization reduction time and space are polynomial and $CM\le(T+1)S$; actual decision and all subsequent DAG/branch costs are still open. DISJ both-label reduction passes, no nonrelativizing or unrestricted lower-bound conclusion, configuration and simultaneous DTISP obligations for the unknown solver remain open.

Executed DO16 controls: 32 source instances (29 SAT, 3 UNSAT), 3262 exhaustive selector-base words, 861 base deletion witnesses, 4361 3-CNF deletion witnesses, 8722 signed NAE deletion witnesses and singleton local hull scans, 160 source-model lifts, largest compiled registry 1932 owners, 6233672 assertions without discrepancy. Includes Fano and three single-clause (single-budget) neighbors, empty clause and opposite unit controls, and seeded sources. General fixed-girth refinement and full native/portfolio execution, stronger closure, kernel checking, physical peak space and CM were not executed. See \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/54ca0f65924690149d6f1846a544b8247442daa2}{checker} and \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/fd37c5883bf1264b94a59a1e6e4dede117b9dd43}{results}. U-PROGRESS remains OPEN.


\section{A linear repair radius in the genuine four-partition source (BS15)}\label{sec:bs15}
\begin{lemma}[One disagreement can require a linear joint exchange]\label{lem:bs15-radius}
For every positive integer $q$ there is a linear positive NAE-3SAT instance with $n=36q$ variables and four explicitly supplied perfect clause partitions, with individually feasible words $x,y$ for the first and second pairs, respectively, satisfying $\operatorname{dist}(x,y)=1$. The instance is SAT, but every common feasible word $z$ requires
\[
\operatorname{dist}(x,z)+\operatorname{dist}(y,z)\ \ge\ 2n/3-1.
\]
The bound is attained by an explicitly constructible common word. Neither a one-side monotone exchange nor any joint repair changing $o(n)$ original variable arcs is sufficient on this family, even though each side and the joint source have both values in every coordinate. This concerns original variable arcs, not the number of abstract cycles, the complexity of finding a large move, or all possible algorithms.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
Set $L=12q$ and index the $3L$ actual variables by $(i,j)\in\mathbb Z_L\times\mathbb Z_3$. For each $i,j$ take the clause
\[
C_{i,j}=\{(i,j),(i+1,j),(i+1,j+1)\}
\]
in partition $(i+j)\bmod3$. Every variable occurs in exactly one clause of each of the first three partitions: its outgoing clause and two incoming clauses have the three distinct partition labels. Distinct clauses share at most one variable, since the only within-layer pairs are the three distinct pairs in a layer and cross-layer pairs have a unique outgoing center.

For the fourth partition use each block of 12 successive layers separately and each fixed $j$ separately. The layer triples for the ordinary $j=0,1$ template are $(0,2,5),(1,3,8),(4,7,10),(6,9,11)$; for $j=2$ use $(0,2,5),(1,3,10),(4,7,9),(6,8,11)$. In block zero for $j=0$ replace its template by $(0,4,8),(1,3,6),(2,9,11),(5,7,10)$. Each template partitions the 12 layers and has no consecutive layer pair even modulo 12, so no fourth-partition clause repeats any pair in the first three partitions. All four partitions are therefore genuine and linear.

Let $x_{i,j}=i\bmod2$. Every boundary clause is legal, and only the special fourth-partition triple $(0,4,8)$ at $j=0$ is monochromatic. Let $y$ differ only at $(0,0)$. Its outgoing clause is in partition zero; its two incoming clauses (partitions one and two) remain legal because just one majority bit changes. The formerly bad fourth-partition clause becomes legal. Thus $x$ is feasible for partitions zero and one, $y$ for partitions two and three, but neither is a common solution. S21's paired-partition flow compiler gives explicit individually feasible unit-width circulations with exactly this disagreement.

Any common word $z$ must differ from $x$ at some variable in the special fourth-partition clause. If it differs at $(i,j)$, the originally minority bit in $C_{i,j}$ is changed, so at least one of $(i+1,j)$ and $(i+1,j+1)$ must also differ from $x$ or that clause becomes monochromatic. Iteration around the ring proves at least one changed variable in every layer, giving $\operatorname{dist}(x,z)\ge L$. The triangle inequality with $\operatorname{dist}(x,y)=1$ gives $\operatorname{dist}(y,z)\ge L-1$. For equality take
\[
z_{i,j}=x_{i,j}\oplus [j=i\bmod3].
\]
Exactly one bit changes per layer; its minority outgoing clause has one changed majority bit, and other outgoing clauses cannot have both majority bits changed. This satisfies every boundary clause. Direct inspection of the four explicitly listed templates verifies all fourth-partition clauses, including the special one. The seed $(0,0)$ changes, so the claimed lower bound is attained. Global complementation preserves every positive NAE relation, hence there are no unconditional coordinate pins in either feasible side or the joint source. No new guard is introduced by this construction.
\end{proof}

\paragraph{Consequences for U-PROGRESS.}
The S21 repair potential $1+\operatorname{dist}(x,y)$ has initial value 2 on this SAT family, yet a successful step must change at least $2n/3-1$ labelled variable-arc values. Therefore one cannot bound the size of a cooperative move by a fixed function of the current disagreement count, or by a fixed-radius exchange, even on the exact NP-complete four-partition geometry. A one-side move preserving all agreements cannot succeed. This falsifies the proposed radius-based acquisition shortcut, not the conditional S21 theorem: its allowed per-call polynomial work can pay a linear-size move. The family itself has a simple explicit witness; a bounded-width decomposition along the ring also bypasses it. It is not a simultaneous hard sequence and no lower bound for unrestricted SAT follows. The number of changed arcs is not a lower bound on the number of cycles in every possible residual decomposition. Discovery of a valid nonlocal exchange on arbitrary four-partition input, or another uniform normalization, remains OPEN.

\paragraph{Executed finite controls and ledger.}
The BS15 checker validates the actual partition and pair incidence identities, both flow bounds and conservation, both-label decision controls, witness lifts and unit-difference cycle decomposition. Seven SAT sizes $n=36,72,144,288,576,1152,2304$ attain the exact radius. A 9-variable affine plane of order three is UNSAT by exhaustive evaluation of all 512 words and has a supplied one-disagreement pair of individually feasible flows; its two feasible sides each have 102 words. Fano and its 21 single-sign neighbors reproduce 0 and 10 models, respectively (source regression only, not a four-partition adapter). 96,113 assertions passed. The complement controls implement singleton coordinate-support closure on the explicit known SAT family; no complete semantic closure or generic polynomial repair search was executed. The finite negative control is exhaustively decided, not an implementation of a general terminal. The construction, exact explicit witness and flow lifts have polynomial bit size and can be acquired in polynomial time for this family; the naive cycle extraction in the checker also has polynomial cost (at most polynomial scans of explicit arc lists). General source acquisition, capped repair calls, failed calls, guard retention, structural key checks, consumer accesses and reconstruction remain subject to Theorem~\ref{thm:aggregate} and the S21 ledger. In the conditional serial algorithm $CM\le(T+1)S$; physical memory was not measured. See \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/61486225f8ce8115f8735dd8e069ad986cd12164}{checker} and \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/16844049d48e0c0ecaa33e402f45f5d20670cba4}{results}.


\section{Exponential flat cut coverage on genuine cooperative repairs (GD22)}\label{sec:gd22}
S21 gives a strictly decreasing Hamming reserve, and BS15 (Lemma~\ref{lem:bs15-radius}) supplies a connected genuine four-partition instance with one disagreement. The following falsifies a proposed full-support acquisition shortcut on that same geometry, even allowing different polarities and arbitrary auxiliary variables in different cut components. It does not exclude finding one valid repair by a different polynomial algorithm.

\begin{lemma}[Flat submodular repair-cover obstruction]\label{lem:gd22-cover}
For the BS15 ring of $q\ge2$ blocks ($n=36q$), fix either value of the unique disagreed coordinate in the common final word. Any exact union of nonempty families of legal common words, each closed under coordinatewise meet and join after its own fixed coordinate complements, has at least $(3/2)^{q-1}$ families. In particular this holds for flat unions of coordinate projections of global minimizer families of submodular functions (including nonnegative-capacity directed $s$-$t$ cuts), with arbitrary private auxiliary variables and piece-specific complements, if each projected piece is contained in the actual legal repair relation. This is not a claim about arbitrary sublevel sets or arbitrary affine decoding.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
With one initial disagreement every strict improvement is a common feasible word. Conversely, any common word lifts to feasible flows on both sides and the entire residual difference decomposes into arc-disjoint cycles, as in Lemma~\ref{lem:s21-cycle}; augmenting the whole batch repairs the seed. Thus full support of monotone batch endpoints equals the common source support. Flip variables relative to the initial words only complement actual coordinates.

Use the BS15 periodic witness in the exceptional block zero. Its first and last layers, in increasing $j$, are $100$ and $110$. In each ordinary block choose one of the following explicit words (indices within each block are zero-based in layer-major order):
\[
\begin{array}{c|l}
(z_6,z_9,z_{10})&\text{36-bit word}\\ \hline
001&\texttt{100001011010100001010100101001010110}\\
010&\texttt{100001010100001011010100011100011110}\\
011&\texttt{100001010110001011010100011100011110}\\
100&\texttt{100011110001110001010100101001010110}\\
101&\texttt{100011100011100011010100101001010110}\\
110&\texttt{100001110100001011010100011100011110}
\end{array}
\]
Substitution in the three boundary and ordinary fourth-partition templates of BS15 verifies every row, with the same first and last layers $100,110$. Hence independently choosing any row per ordinary block preserves all seams. This gives seed value one; global complement gives seed value zero. The selected triple $(6,9,10)$ in each ordinary block is an actual clause, so projection onto these $3(q-1)$ coordinates is exactly $\operatorname{NAE}_3^{q-1}$ on each pinned seed fiber. The underlying source is connected: within each layer, the successor pairs of the preceding layer connect all three variables, and consecutive layers are linked around the ring.

A global minimizer set of a submodular function is a lattice by the submodular inequality and minimality, and fixing coordinates or projecting to coordinates preserves meet and join closure. Coordinate complements simply choose an orientation. In any orientation $\operatorname{NAE}_3$ excludes two complementary words. A lattice subset has at most four words: for excluded $001,110$, the disjoint pairs $(011,101)$ and $(010,100)$ respectively meet and join to forbidden words, so at least one word from each pair must be omitted. Permutation and global complement cover all mixed exclusions. For excluded $000,111$, use pairs $(001,010)$ and $(101,110)$. Every oriented lattice piece projected to the selected triples consequently has at most $4^{q-1}$ points; this bound holds even if those triples are correlated. Exact cover of all $6^{q-1}$ points requires at least $(6/4)^{q-1}$ pieces. The explicit source length is polynomial in $q$, so enumerating these pieces cannot fit fixed polynomial occurrence credit even if local piece acquisition is free.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[Factored guarded seam repair on the same family]\label{lem:gd22-seam}
Given the authenticated BS15 block ordering, both seed-fiber feasibility and a witness are computable in polynomial bit time and space, without flattening the cover in Lemma~\ref{lem:gd22-cover}.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
Use the first layer of each block as an actual three-bit seam. Enumerate each block's private assignments conditional on its first layer and the next block's first layer, at most 64 seam pairs and a constant 39 Boolean coordinates per local query. Include all clauses within the block and its outgoing boundary clauses, and the real seed pin in block zero. Retain a witness for each feasible pair. For each of eight possible initial seams, perform eight-state reachability through the successive pair tables and require the last outgoing seam to equal the initial one. The witnesses from a successful path glue exactly, since all interblock clauses inspect only these seams. Conversely every global model supplies such a path. Charge local discovery, unsuccessful queries, pin closure, guard provenance, seam accesses, predecessor records, reconstruction and original source verification. The same procedure can retain all local accepted words as edge labels of a polynomial-size factored DAG, without enumerating its paths; no claim of cheap universal discovery of such a plan. All per-block local costs are bounded by a constant times polynomial encoding overhead, and serial composition and storage are polynomial in original $N$, including naive path-copy overhead. Thus $CM\le(T+1)S$ on this realization.
\end{proof}

\paragraph{Implication for U-PROGRESS.}
An exact flat union of cut-minimizer families is not a universally polynomial representation of repair support even on the true four-partition source and with one disagreement. The guarded seam construction is a paid nonlocal bypass on this adversarial family. It does not provide universal discovery; a decision-only procedure can instead select just one valid move. Accordingly the positive-promise acquisition obligation in S21 and Obligation~\ref{obl:progress} remain OPEN. There is no unrestricted lower bound or implication of $\mathrm P\ne\mathrm{NP}$. See Kolmogorov--Zabih, \href{https://doi.org/10.1109/TPAMI.2004.1262177}{PAMI 2004}, for graph-representability context; the lattice argument here is self-contained.

Executed GD22 finite controls: 13,805 assertions, 3,050 local closure scans, 1,617 finite DPLL calls, 192 seam table queries (including pin-incompatible skips), ordinary table 50 feasible pairs, exceptional tables 24 each. Six SAT sizes through 1152 variables, both seed fibers solved by actual DP; 708 checked concatenated/complemented witnesses; all 8 orientations and 64 subsets tested. A genuine 9-variable affine-plane one-mismatch UNSAT control has zero models among 512 words, with both seed fibers independently rejected by DPLL. Only singleton coordinate closure implemented, not full affine or semantic closure. General repair search, actual residual cycle extraction, complete portfolio, native replay, formal kernel and physical CM not executed. See \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/e028a71af63e85addc063db51f0ab934367a1bb2}{checker}, \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/3ee57e0eb6c901a8f565d9c800e202f67611592e}{results} and \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/1ebb1494d28732b6657a3990c262ca3e899c8d1f}{report}.


\section{Guard discharge and global bounded-degree closure (DA16)}\label{sec:da16}
Obligation~\ref{obl:progress} cannot be filled by merely upgrading the DA15 singleton and BA15 quadratic-polar closure to a fixed-degree polynomial-calculus saturation and allowing arbitrary dense affine branch forms. The following theorem attacks an arbitrary binary affine case tree, rather than only the specified separated-anchor dispatch of DP16. It also gives a constructive closure procedure which refutes two of the common small obstructions without branching. The claims are restricted to certificates in the stated proof calculus.

\begin{lemma}[Affine guard discharge and logarithmic leaf bound]\label{lem:da16-discharge}
Let $P$ be a set of Boolean equations over $\mathbb F_2$. Use Boolean polynomial calculus (BPC), with squarefree polynomials, XOR and multiplication by a variable followed by Boolean reduction. Degree is the maximum reduced degree of a proof line. If $P\cup\{g=0\}$ has a degree-$d$ BPC refutation for an affine form $g$ and $d\ge1$, then $P$ derives $1+g=0$ in degree at most $d+1$.

If a finite binary affine case tree refutes $P$, every leaf being equipped with a BPC refutation from $P$ and its actual path equations of degree at most $D$, and the tree has $L$ leaf occurrences, then $P$ has a BPC refutation of degree at most $D+\lfloor\log_2 L\rfloor$. This includes arbitrary adaptive dense affine branch forms and arbitrary tree depth. Any preprocessing consequences used by a leaf must be included as derivations at the stated degree cap in the original coordinate ring.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
Multiply every line of the first refutation by $1+g$. The added guard axiom maps to zero by Boolean reduction; every other axiom times $1+g$ is obtained from that axiom by variable multiplications and XOR. Both inference rules commute with multiplication by $1+g$ in the Boolean ring, and degree increases by at most one. The final constant one maps to $1+g$. The other guard value is symmetric. Induct on the case tree: discharge the smaller child first, whose leaf count is at most $L/2$, then use its derived opposite guard as an already established equation in the larger child refutation. The smaller child incurs one additional degree, the larger incurs none. This yields the stated logarithmic bound, including highly unbalanced trees. Ordinary polynomial calculus simulates BPC with degree overhead at most one: after multiplying a multilinear polynomial by a variable, replace squared-variable terms by their squarefree versions using monomial multiples of the Boolean axioms. All these operations are formal proof transformations; they do not assume a semantic oracle.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[Degree transfer to strict linear signed NAE cores]\label{lem:da16-transfer}
There exist signed linear ternary NAE registries with maximum variable degree four, $O(n)$ variables and owners, and BPC refutation degree $\Omega(n)$, where $n$ is the original source variable count. They may be chosen inclusion-minimal UNSAT, and hence strict rank-Hall cores as in Lemma~\ref{lem:ds15-residual}, with stagnant root singleton, same-plane and BA15 constant-coefficient polar closure. Each single forbidden-word change has a SAT neighbor on the same observation planes. No uniform efficient generator of minimal cores or all deletion witnesses is asserted.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
Use Mik\v{s}a--Nordstr\"om, \emph{A Generalized Method for Proving Polynomial Calculus Degree Lower Bounds}, Theorem 5.1, \href{https://doi.org/10.1145/3675668}{DOI}, \href{https://arxiv.org/abs/1505.01358}{preprint}: a CNF with $(s,\delta)$ clause-variable boundary expansion requires ordinary PC refutation degree greater than $\delta s/2$ over any field. Boundary expansion means at least $\delta |J|$ variables appearing exactly once in every nonempty clause set $J$ of size at most $s$.

Take $6n$ independent random signed 3-clauses on distinct variables within each clause. The expected number of models is $2^n(7/8)^{6n}=o(1)$. For a set of $t$ clauses to have at most $v=\lfloor7t/4\rfloor$ neighbors, the union bound is
\[
 {6n\choose t}{n\choose v}(v/n)^{3t}
 \le [C(t/n)^{1/4}]^t
\]
for an absolute constant $C$, by the usual binomial coefficient upper bounds. Choose constant $\alpha>0$ with $C\alpha^{1/4}<1/2$ and sum over $t\le\alpha n$, splitting at $\sqrt n$; the sum tends to zero. With high probability all such sets have more than $7t/4$ neighbors, hence more than $t/2$ boundary variables by counting their $3t$ incidences. Thus UNSAT and linear ordinary PC degree coexist on explicit linear-size sources. Truth-value convention is immaterial by affine substitution.

Replace each clause $\ell_1\vee\ell_2\vee\ell_3$ by
\[
 \operatorname{NAE}(\ell_1,\ell_2,y),\quad
 \operatorname{NAE}(1+y,\ell_3,z),
\]
with private $y$ per clause and one shared phase $z$. Complement symmetry permits $z=0$. The gadget is equisatisfiable with the clause, with a valid lift $y=(1+\ell_1)(1+\ell_2)$. For arbitrary source words, under this substitution and $z=0$ the first rank-two violation polynomial is identically zero and the second is precisely the clause violation polynomial $\prod_{j=1}^3(1+\ell_j)$. Thus any degree-$d$ BPC refutation substitutes to a source BPC refutation of degree at most $2d+2$: simulate a variable multiplication by expanding its substituted polynomial of degree at most two into monomials, multiply termwise and XOR. All intermediate degrees are at most $2d+2$, and the ordinary PC overhead is at most one. The lower bound therefore transfers.

Now use the switched six-line Fano equality gadget from Lemma~\ref{lem:bh15-repair}. Separate every occurrence into an external leaf; each variable with $t$ occurrences has $t$ gadgets in a chain, with terminals consisting of consecutive backbone vertices and the corresponding occurrence leaf. Each equality gadget has four private internal vertices. The two constant assignments are legal and all terminal values must agree. The chain is exact, with maximum degree four and a linear triple registry: successive gadgets share at most one backbone vertex and each external triple meets a gadget in at most one leaf. Substitute all occurrence, backbone and internal bits for each variable by that original variable's degree-at-most-two Boolean substitute. The switched gadget equations then vanish identically; the external equations substitute as above. Thus the same degree simulation holds and the output has linear variable and owner counts. The compiler, lift and verification are uniform polynomial bit work; no proof-size bound is inferred from the small source representation.

Choose an inclusion-minimal UNSAT subregistry existentially. Any refutation of the subregistry would refute its parent using the same axioms and coordinates, so the degree lower bound persists (unused coordinates can be retained). Minimality gives the strict Hall condition by Lemma~\ref{lem:ds15-core}; linearity gives root singleton, same-plane and polar stagnation by Lemma~\ref{lem:ba15-core}. For each owner a deletion model violates precisely that owner and therefore satisfies any different forbidden word replacement. This establishes opposite-label neighbors without an algorithm for obtaining their witnesses. This is not asserted to be the particular DO09 or MS21 family, and no native E4/B4 degree simulation, growing-girth construction or simultaneous obstruction against PSD/flow/matching follows.
\end{proof}

\paragraph{Aggregate consequence and paid constructive feedback.}
On the family of Lemma~\ref{lem:da16-transfer}, any affine binary case tree with BPC leaf refutations bounded by $D$ must have $L\ge 2^{\Omega(n)-D}$. In particular no polynomial numerical potential of Theorem~\ref{thm:aggregate} pays for this restricted tree dispatch if $D=o(n)$, even with arbitrary branch order, dense query basis, and any such potential. With ordinary integer identifiers the encoded length is $O(n\log n)$; the leaf lower bound is superpolynomial in that length for fixed $D$. This conclusion does not cover arbitrary factored DAGs with exponentially many unfolded paths, same-context proof reuse, non-PC decision certificates, or nonlocal transformations without a bounded-degree proof lift. It is a proof-system restriction, not a lower bound on unrestricted decision time, and the diagonal lemma still applies only to a fixed total solver.

For fixed $D$ there is a paid complete saturation in the specified degree: maintain the Gaussian span in the $\sum_{j=0}^D{n\choose j}$-dimensional squarefree monomial basis. For each variable, compute the linear image of the span under multiplication, intersect with the degree-$D$ subspace by eliminating high coordinates, and add all resulting vectors. Merely multiplying individual basis vectors of small degree would not implement the stated calculus. Each strict enlargement increases dimension, so the procedure is polynomial for fixed $D$, including acquisition and verification of derivations. Necessary affine rows can be added as certified deductions with actual guards retained; closure is rerun after deductions and at stagnation. No complete semantic hull is free. For one fixed-degree job the serial same-execution $T,S$ are polynomial and $CM\le(T+1)S$; this does not pay for the entire exponential tree. A nonlocal identity, joint occupancy/circulation certificate, or sound factored sharing may bypass the restricted tree; its actual acquisition and lift costs still require proof.

Executed DA16 controls on the common Fano and genuine 9-variable four-partition affine plane: both UNSAT roots retain full ambient charts under singleton hull closure and stall at BPC degree two but refute at degree three. All 21 single-sign Fano neighbors (10 models each) and 14 single-budget neighbors (5 each) were not refuted at tested degrees. For all 63 nonzero relative Fano parity masks, seven have both degree-two child refutations and 56 have neither, consistent with guard discharge. The 64 signed clause/word combinations verified the compiler substitution; the switched Fano gadget had ten legal words, all terminal-equal. Ledger: 384 assertions, 184 singleton closure scans, 21 strict hull changes, 4499 span insertions, 388 saturation rounds and 55396 basis products. The finite hull checker enumerates tiny ambient charts, not a general polynomial implementation. No large degree-hard family generated, actual discharged proof trace materialized, full portfolio, formal kernel or measured physical CM. See \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/98aa613b0d885868fee92a3fe61fdc6d42b1802e}{DA16 report}, \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/d91f769acbfb1a9cd6a4cbbfe67573581472ea9c}{checker}, and \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/dfe615cff36377cb525e6cab882cd846b0bebe09}{results}.

\paragraph{Joint stagnation request.}
On the identical Fano and four-partition plane, and separately on the explicitly mapped degree-hard compiler family, compare the BA15/DA16 deductions with actual matching and circulation witnesses, wheel synchronization, multisign guarded DAG and exact seam/factor certificates. Keep source maps and guards; charge consumer and proof lift costs, rerun closure after each accepted deduction, and distinguish a non-PC terminal from a proof of universal bounded-degree closure. The next actual missing step remains one uniform exact contraction or decision with polynomial aggregate acquisition and sharing, not a larger fixed-degree cap. U-PROGRESS and unconditional COST remain OPEN.


\section{Simultaneous paired-flow closure fixed point on the actual repair source (M22)}\label{sec:m22}
This attacks the load-bearing acquisition in S21 (Lemma~\ref{lem:s21-cycle}), not the general signed-network reduction. BS15 (Lemma~\ref{lem:bs15-radius}) and GD22 (Lemma~\ref{lem:gd22-seam}) supply a common authenticated SAT benchmark; the affine plane of order three supplies an UNSAT benchmark with a one-mismatch pair. The following rules out a proposed universal progress guarantee for the combined exact side convex and affine-lattice normalizations, even across all three pairings of the four partitions.

\begin{lemma}[Simultaneous full side support under one seed pin]\label{lem:m22-hull}
Let $P_0,\dots,P_3$ be perfect partitions of the same $n$ actual Boolean variables into positive NAE triples. For each of the six unordered partition pairs let $X_i$ be the exact side support of legal Boolean words. Let $g$ be the empty guard or one actual pin $x_s=b$ on every synchronized copy. Then (i) the exact convex hulls of all guarded $X_i$ contain a common relative neighborhood in the free coordinate space; (ii) every actual guarded side word occurs with positive mass in some collection of side distributions with equal means; (iii) each guarded $X_i$ generates the entire integer affine lattice on its free coordinates, and its binary affine hull is likewise full. In particular simultaneous convex-face/support-restricted affine-integer filtering with equality of all six projected words as the target deletes no actual side option. Alternating these particular filters and their implied affine deductions reaches a fixed point without a repair or contradiction certificate, even on UNSAT. No conclusion is asserted for exact nonlinear joins or higher-order moments.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
For each pair, form a cubic bipartite graph whose vertices are clauses and whose edges are actual variables. Legal side words are precisely edge subsets with degree one or two at every vertex. Their convex hull consists of $0\le x_e\le1$ and vertex sums in $[1,2]$, by the integral circulation representation in S21 (integral bounds retain integrality after a pin). The common rational point $p$ is $1/2$ on every free coordinate at the root, $3/5$ under $b=0$, and $2/5$ under $b=1$. Set the pinned coordinate to $b$. Every free coordinate and every triple sum has strict slack: the sums are $3/2$, or $6/5$ or $9/5$. Hence $p$ belongs to the relative interior of every guarded hull. For any actual guarded side word $a$, the vector $q=(20p-a)/19$ satisfies the same side bounds and sums (the pinned coordinate is unchanged, all clause numerators between 19 and 38). Thus $p=(a+19q)/20$ is a convex combination with positive mass on $a$, using integrality to express $q$ as a mixture of actual side words. All other sides can realize the same mean $p$. This also preserves every transition occurring in any full side derivation if a convex-flow formulation is used: that derivation can receive positive mass.

Each cubic bipartite graph has three disjoint perfect matching color classes, obtained by successive Hall matchings. For a free edge $f$, select a color class containing neither $f$ nor the pinned edge $s$ (if present); at most two colors are excluded. Its incidence word $u$ and $u+e_f$ are both legal because adding an absent edge increases just its endpoint degrees from one to two. Complement both words for $b=1$. Their difference is a signed coordinate unit, proving the integer lattice assertion; reduction modulo two gives the affine hull assertion. For each fixed side word, every other side can therefore match its projection by an integer affine combination of legal guarded side words, with coefficients summing to one but possibly negative. Such combinations use only options that also pass the rational support test. This proves simultaneous survival, and repeating the specified filters cannot shrink these supports. Return-arc coordinates in the circulation are uniquely determined by clause sums, and any implied linear equalities there can be recorded without changing the projected support. Semantic forced-information closure on the exact joint Boolean relation is not inferred.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[No favorable flat quota basis for the seeded synchronization]\label{lem:m22-defect}
For any pairing into two sides, under either value of a single seed, write the exact integer synchronization condition as $x-y=0$. In the flat M19 extremum-defect normalization, any injective integer quota transformation of the free discrepancy coordinates has defect-product parameter $B\ge2^{n-1}$, even after valid coordinatewise GCD normalization. At the unguarded root the analogous bound is $2^n$.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
By Lemma~\ref{lem:m22-hull}, both guarded hulls contain a common relative neighborhood of $p$. The convex hull of all independent side discrepancies therefore contains a relative neighborhood of zero in the free coordinate space. Each nonzero integer functional on that space has strictly negative and positive attainable integer extrema. After any valid division by a common positive integer divisor of the attainable discrepancies, the extrema remain integers of opposite sign. The defect from target zero in either orientation is at least one. Injectivity on the free discrepancy space requires at least its dimension in nonzero rows. The product of one plus each defect is consequently at least two to that dimension.
\end{proof}

\paragraph{Implications for the constructive spine.}
At one mismatch a successful S21 monotone improvement is exactly a common Boolean word. The two possible actual seed pins exhaust the target. Under either pin the convex relaxation has zero mismatch optimum, and the combined affine/lattice filters cannot delete side words, regardless of the initial feasible pair. This obstruction applies to the genuine NP-complete four-partition source, not just general unit-width networks. It does not rule out target-free nonlinear cooperative moves, exact matching with additional information, or GD22's factored seam bypass. The defect bound is a lower bound on the stated parameter, not on the number of actual reachable states or unrestricted time. S21's Hamming credit remains valid conditional on acquiring a move; U-PROGRESS and unconditional COST remain OPEN.

\paragraph{Charged acquisition and collaborative residual.}
The six colorings are uniformly acquired by polynomial augmenting-path searches. At most $O(n)$ unit-difference pairs per side and guard, $O(n^2)$ explicit certificate bits, suffice; the rational witness formula has constant denominators. Authenticate partition incidence, source map, actual pin guards, each pair, and clause sums. Perform these bounded certified side-coordinate/affine/lattice closure scans initially and for the guarded residuals, repeating after any accepted deduction; no extra strict deletion occurs from these methods alone. No branch or full support enumeration is required for the theorem. Time and peak space of these certificates are polynomial on the same serial execution and $CM\le(T+1)S$; exact joint acquisition remains unpaid. Coordinate numbering $3i+j$, BS15 block zero exception and seed zero on both copies, plus the four line classes of $\mathbb F_3^2$, are the common authenticated benchmarks. Request nonlinear algebra/multisign deductions, exact wheel/interface seam transfers, factored guarded DAG coverage, matching/solver joint move search and adversarial structural/aggregate audits on those identical guarded instances. Apply accepted deductions across tracks and repeat closure; no unexecuted peer contribution is assumed. Compare standalone versus combined progress, retaining all guards. Neither a complete semantic closure oracle nor universal branch credit is assumed.

Executed M22 controls: all three pairings, six sides, root and both seed pins for affine plane (512 exhaustive words, zero common, one mismatch found) and BS15 $q=1,2,4,8$ (36--288 variables, explicit common witness). 90 guard scans, 9,822 actual unit-difference pairs, 20,568 exact rational numerator checks (all side words for plane, constructed pairs for rings), 120,667 assertions without discrepancy. Source partitions and linearity checked. No general LP backend, target-free repair, native replay, formal kernel, actual cycle extraction or measured CM executed. See \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/2471b7d365f9f9f77f893e722b553b4f6a06464a}{report}, \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/2163b8eec088a28dab4ff368f5b49465b71cc006}{checker} and \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/a18db5a4183c3a4674a213ebc927bf237c1d396c}{results}. Source complexity verified against Darmann--D\"ocker--Dorn, \href{https://arxiv.org/html/2412.03395v3}{arXiv:2412.03395v3}; no extension to constrained weighted three-partition decision assumed.


\section{Guard-weakening DAG discharge without unfolding (DR16)}\label{sec:dr16}
This addresses the open DAG qualification of Lemma~\ref{lem:da16-discharge} and the provenance requirement of DP16. It does not assume that a successful graph can be discovered.

\begin{lemma}[Bounded-rank affine guard DAG degree]\label{lem:dr16-dag}
Fix a common original Boolean polynomial ring over $\mathbb F_2$ and source axioms $P$. Let a finite acyclic refutation graph have true root guard and consistent affine node guards of rank at most $r$. An internal node with guard $G$ queries an affine form $q$; each consistent branch $B=G\cap\{q=b\}$ points to a child with guard $C$ satisfying $B\subseteq C$ as actual affine subspaces, independently of $P$. Inconsistent branches can be checked by Gaussian elimination. Children may be shared and may forget earlier equations. Every leaf has a BPC refutation from $P$ and its guard in degree at most $D\ge1$, including all preprocessing derivations used in the original ring. Then $P$ has a BPC refutation of degree at most $D+r$, irrespective of the number of nodes, depth or unfolded paths.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
For a consistent affine guard $G$ with independent equations $g_j=0$, write $I_G=\prod_j(1+g_j)$ in the Boolean ring. This is its unique multilinear indicator, of degree at most $\operatorname{rank}G$, independent of the chosen basis. For a leaf, multiply each proof line by $I_G$. Every original axiom times $I_G$ is derived by successive affine multiplications (expand by variable multiplications and XOR), and each added guard axiom times $I_G$ is zero. Multiplication and XOR commute with this map. All resulting BPC lines have reduced degree at most $D+r$. The final line derives $I_G=0$.

Process nodes in reverse topological order. For a consistent branch $B\subseteq C$, the independent affine equations defining $C$ lie in the affine equation span defining $B$. Extend them to a basis for $B$ using additional equations $h_j=0$. Hence $I_B=I_C\prod_j(1+h_j)$. From the already derived $I_C=0$, successive affine multiplications derive $I_B=0$ at degree at most $\operatorname{rank}B\le r+1$. The inconsistent branch has zero indicator. Since the branch indicators partition $G$, their XOR is $I_G$, giving $I_G=0$. At the root $I_G=1$. Each child indicator is derived once and reused at its consumers; no path unfolding is needed. Since $D\ge1$, the maximum degree is at most $D+r$.
\end{proof}

\paragraph{Consequence and constructive scope.}
On the degree-hard family of Lemma~\ref{lem:da16-transfer}, any such DAG must satisfy $D+r=\Omega(n)$. Bounded-degree leaves and logarithmic-rank guards therefore cannot refute this family even with unrestricted graph size and sound weakening, let alone a polynomial graph. This is a restriction on the combined proof model, not on unrestricted SAT, arbitrary factored DAGs, nonaffine state interfaces, extension variables, source-dependent guard implications, or non-PC certificates without the stated degree lift. Two distinct feasible paths in an exact affine decision tree are disjoint at their first divergent query, so identical full-path guards alone cannot supply cross-path sharing. A justified weakening or different authenticated interface is necessary.

For fixed $D,r$, an explicitly supplied polynomial-size graph and polynomial-size leaf traces admit polynomial-cost expanded proof verification: the degree cap bounds each line by $O(n^{D+r})$ monomials, and Gaussian edge implication, basis extension, affine multiplication, each consumer and every proof line are charged. Growing rank may require large polynomial expansions; the degree bound is not a bit-size bound. No universal graph/trace acquisition is established. Certified local closure is applied initially, after each accepted deduction and at stagnation, with conditional deductions retained in actual guards. Complete semantic closure is not assumed. On the stated fixed-cap explicit certificate domain serial time and peak space are polynomial and $CM\le(T+1)S$; the universal resource ledger is still open.

\paragraph{Shared benchmark and adversarial audit.}
Using DA16's actual Fano and $9$-variable affine plane registries (identity source map), construct a five-node DAG: root queries $x_0$, its two children query $q$, and both share the two leaves with guard $q=b$ alone. Fano $q=x_1+x_2+x_3+x_4$ (mask 30): both degree-two leaf checks refute, root degree two stalls and degree three refutes. Plane $q=x_0+x_1$ (mask 3): both degree-three leaf checks refute and degree three already refutes the root. Thus shared reuse saves two terminal occurrences in the unfolded graph, but does not improve on the direct global degree-three terminal for these controls. All 21 single-sign Fano neighbors have ten models and neither of their degree-two leaves is refuted. The executed checker verifies 12,640 indicator/implication identities, 12,791 assertions, 101 singleton closure scans, 11 strict changes, 1,804 span insertions, 181 rounds and 22,959 basis products. Tiny closure uses exhaustive reference hulls; BPC checks use the original axioms and actual guards only. Full trace materialization, general proof kernel, large hard family and complete portfolio not executed. See \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/ab4d54a0ed28fe9c781409ff797192fd835b8b60}{checker} and \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/95b0a9f9994f90acb06af2e7bf2db9b9ec28a689}{results}. The relevant known regular resolution-over-parities bound (Efremenko, Garlik, Itsykson, \href{https://doi.org/10.1137/24M1696640}{DOI}) does not supply an arbitrary DAG-size lower bound.

\paragraph{Joint stagnation protocol.}
U-PROGRESS remains OPEN. On the same authenticated Fano and plane, plus M22/BS15 rings under their actual seed guards and separately DA16's degree-hard compiler family, request non-PC or high-degree factored identities from algebra/hubs, exact nonlinear occupancy/circulation certificates from matching/interfaces, native source lifts from wheels, nonaffine or extension-state sharing from multisign/guarded DAG, aggregate counterexamples from obstruction/potential, and combined closure and paid discovery from solver/synthesis. Preserve maps and guards, compare standalone versus combined progress, reclose after each accepted deduction and charge all consumers. No unexecuted peer contribution is claimed. The plane's M22 side fixed point is bypassed by DA16 global degree-three closure, not by complete marginal support analysis. The current theorem identifies a genuine shared-model barrier, not a universal simultaneous obstruction.


\section{Degree-audited proof transport across synchronized strict hubs (BW16)}\label{sec:bw16}
This closes the specific native proof-degree transfer left open by Lemma~\ref{lem:da16-transfer}; it does not close U-PROGRESS. The M22 exact paired-flow marginal fixed point is bypassed on the common affine plane by a DA16 degree-three joint contradiction, which can be lifted to the actual I21 physical graph without assuming free semantic closure.

\begin{lemma}[Affine chart and native BPC proof transport]\label{lem:bw16-transport}
For an explicit signed E4/B4 graph, let $L$ be the signed wire, E4 equality, B4 even-parity and actual affine guard equations over $\mathbb F_2$. Use at each B4 the additional quadratic axiom
\[
 Q_i=(1+p_0+p_1)(1+p_0+p_2)=0.
\]
If $L$ is consistent, take its Gaussian chart $p=Ax+b$ with $x$ chosen as free actual port coordinates, retaining $q_i=Q_i(Ax+b)$ as explicit chart axioms. This is an exact guarded decision and witness adapter. A chart BPC refutation of degree $d$ gives a native refutation of degree at most $\max(2,d)$; a native refutation of degree $d$ gives a chart refutation of degree at most $\max(2,d+1)$. If $L$ is inconsistent a degree-one refutation suffices. These simulations can be implemented with polynomial bit work in the graph and explicit proof length for every fixed degree bound.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
On the even-parity four-bit subspace the quadratic is one precisely on the two constant words, so its vanishing is equivalent to integer balance two. Gaussian parameterization preserves all actual guards and constraints in both directions, and lifts a chart model by evaluating the port expressions and verifying the source.

For the pullback, substitute the affine expressions into every proof line in the squarefree Boolean ring of DA16. Each linear axiom becomes zero and each native quadratic becomes its chart axiom. XOR commutes with substitution. A variable multiplication becomes multiplication by an affine form: derive each individual variable product of the substituted premise and XOR these products and the possible constant multiple. Individual terms have degree at most $d+1$, even when high-degree terms cancel in the final product. This is a proof transformation on polynomials, not an argument that assumes a satisfying word.

For the lift, Gaussian elimination records an XOR derivation from $L$ of every $p_j+(Ax+b)_j=0$ in the original port ring, where each $x$ is the actual selected free port. Write $Q_i=UV$ and $q_i=U'V'$ with affine factors. Then
\[
 Q_i+q_i=(U+U')V+U'(V+V').
\]
The two affine differences are consequences in the row span of $L$. Their products are derivable in degree at most two by variable multiplication and XOR; adding $Q_i$ gives the lifted chart axiom. Subsequent chart multiplications use actual free ports, preserving degree. One may equivalently use signed representative ports $p_j+s$ for a source coordinate: adding the premise in a multiplication by $p_j+s$ cannot cancel a new degree-$d+1$ term, so the separate multiplication by $p_j$ remains within the degree cap if the substituted premise and output have degree at most $d$. Fixed-degree dense expansions have polynomial monomial count, and row provenance, intermediate products and all proof-line consumers are charged.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[Native affine-extension and unfolded-tree obstruction]\label{lem:bw16-tree}
Under the I21 direct signed NAE compiler (before any 3-CNF-to-NAE stage), the degree-hard NAE families of Lemma~\ref{lem:da16-transfer} transfer to simple loopless signed four-regular E4/B4 graphs with $O(n)$ vertices and wires, requiring BPC refutation degree $\Omega(n)$, where $n$ is the source variable count in that lemma. If an affine binary case tree on physical ports has leaf refutations of degree at most $D$ in the original port ring from native axioms and actual path guards, its unfolded leaf count $L_{\rm tree}$ satisfies
\[
 L_{\rm tree}\ge 2^{\Omega(n)-D-1}
\]
for sufficiently large $n$. In particular fixed-degree physical closure with an arbitrary affine branch tree does not yield universal polynomial occurrence credit. More generally, a degree-$d$ refutation of an affine extension whose axioms pull back to source consequences with degree-at-most-$D_0$ derivations yields a source refutation of degree at most $\max(D_0,d+1)$.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
For I21, assign each B4 occurrence its actual signed literal and its fourth bit the XOR of the three signed literals, identically on both duplicates. The latter is affine on every source word, not only on satisfying words. Each E4 path and private $K_5$-minus-edge wire gadget receives its prescribed common affine bit. Thus every linear native axiom pulls back to zero and each quadratic to the original NAE violation polynomial. The reverse selection of a signed occurrence per variable gives the lift of Lemma~\ref{lem:bw16-transport}; the compiler has linear object count. The same substitution argument proves the general extension statement using the supplied derivations of the pulled-back axioms; it does not itself establish equisatisfiability of an arbitrary extension, which must be proved separately. By DA16 guard discharge a native tree with $L_{\rm tree}$ leaves gives a native root proof of degree at most $D+\lfloor\log_2 L_{\rm tree}\rfloor$. Pullback and the source degree lower bound give the asserted inequality. All leaf preprocessing consequences must be derivable within the stated cap in the original port ring; no semantic oracle is allowed.
\end{proof}

\paragraph{Consequences for universal progress and collaboration.}
The proof-degree transfer is a paid way to pass certified global nonlinear deductions between algebra and physical shared hubs. For fixed degree, acquire the chart by Gaussian elimination and use DA16's full degree-bounded saturation (intersect multiplication images with the low-degree subspace, recording preimages before multiplying). The monomial count and the number of strict span enlargements are polynomial for fixed cap. Derive and transport affine necessities only from certificates, reclose initially, after each accepted deduction and at stagnation, preserving actual conditional guards. A contradiction is a paid UNSAT terminal; failure is UNKNOWN, not a SAT decision. A model of the chart has a polynomial verified native lift. All compiler, discovery, verification, row/proof provenance, DAG consumer, failed job and reconstruction costs are paid; on each fixed-degree serial job $T,S$ are polynomial and $CM\le(T+1)S$. The theorem is not a polynomial search claim at unbounded degree. The restricted tree lower bound does not cover factored DAGs with exponentially many unfolded paths, nonaffine gadgets, non-PC identities, or arbitrary decision algorithms. Nor does it identify the DA16 family with the DO09 or M22 families. Hence merely adding affinely synchronized duplicates or changing physical geometry cannot establish universal bounded-degree tree contraction. A polynomial-bit high-degree or non-PC certificate, genuine factored sharing with paid coverage, or a target-free joint repair algorithm is still needed. U-PROGRESS and unconditional COST remain OPEN.

Executed BW16 controls use the common Fano and four-partition plane with IDs $3i+j$, root and actual $x_0=0,1$, plus all 21 Fano single-sign neighbors. The checker builds actual strict graphs including both duplicate B4 copies and all private simple-wire gadgets; verifies port use, simplicity, signed equality paths, parity, and pointwise quadratic pullback; runs finite local hull closure and complete source degree-two/three saturation as specified; records and independently replays source proof lines and their signed representative substitutions. The six root/pin UNSAT charts are stagnant at quotient degree two and rejected at degree three (trace lengths $468,238,249$ Fano and $242,256,256$ plane); by Lemma~\ref{lem:bw16-transport} they admit native degree-three lifts. No claim of native degree-two stagnation follows from the additive-one reverse bound. The 21 sign neighbors have ten models each and are not rejected; the 14 budget neighbors have five models each, checked source-only. This complements M22's all-six-side fixed point and DA16's joint deduction on the identical guarded inputs. Ledger: 33 degree/guard jobs, 211008 assertions, 29348 constructed ports, 14674 wires, 108109 signed path steps, 269 finite closure scans, 1665 span insertions, 14158 image products, 7427 source proof lines, 6528 source/guard words; no discrepancies. The checker does not materialize the full native proof or run native saturation, general repair acquisition, BS15 ring replay, full portfolio, formal kernel verification or measured physical CM. See \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/828faea2d784657c7722bc847e2dac37b3d52548}{report}, \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/92da2a01049b5a91b0d64dbc120ce0c4555bf1a5}{checker}, \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/5bd8415170a1df76c1cbc19828af5e22700b7ce5}{results}.


\begin{lemma}[Concurrent DR16 integration: native bounded-rank guard DAGs]\label{lem:bw16-dag}
On the native family of Lemma~\ref{lem:bw16-tree}, a DR16 refutation DAG over the common physical port ring with actual affine guards of rank at most $r$, source-independent guard weakening along each edge, and BPC leaf degree at most $D$ must satisfy
\[
 D+r+1\ge\Omega(n).
\]
This holds irrespective of the number of distinct nodes or unfolded paths, under precisely the DR16 premises. 
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
DR16 (Lemma~\ref{lem:dr16-dag}) gives a native root refutation of degree at most $D+r$ without unfolding. The I21 affine pullback in Lemma~\ref{lem:bw16-tree} yields a source refutation of degree at most $\max(2,D+r+1)$. Apply DA16's source degree lower bound. All actual guard implications in DR16 must hold as affine implications independent of the source; source-dependent guard implications, nonaffine interfaces, unbounded-degree source axiom lifts, and arbitrary sharing architectures are not covered. In particular a bounded-degree leaf system cannot be made complete merely by adding logarithmic-rank guard sharing to the affine physical compiler.
\end{proof}
This is a joint symbolic consequence of the concurrent DR16 and BW16 sections, not a separate executed large-family experiment. On the small DR16 five-node Fano/plane guarded controls, DR16's leaf traces and the BW16 chart-lift construction supply a native proof path with actual port guards; the root degree-three terminal already suffices. The mask-30/mask-3 shared DAG native replay was not executed in BW16. The precise remaining target is a paid nonlocal certificate or decision mechanism outside these bounded-degree affine transfer premises, not simply a larger fixed cap.

\paragraph{Joint stagnation request.}
Issue \#327 comment 7154585098 requests complementary work by existing peers on the same actual Fano, plane, and BS15 ring with authenticated source maps and seed guards: Algebra/Hub/MS for nonlinear identities with native lift, Matching/Interfaces for occupancy and syndrome provenance, GD for genuine guarded sharing, Solver for comparison and replay, Obstruction/Red Team/Potential/Synthesis for resource and proof-lift audit. Exchange exact deductions, reclose, preserve guards and charge each consumer. No peer contribution beyond the cited results is assumed. No public release.


\section{Source-certified nonlinear weighted DAG discharge (BD16)}\label{sec:bd16}
This extends DR16 (Lemma~\ref{lem:dr16-dag}) to nonaffine guards and source-dependent cancellation, addressing its stated scope restriction rather than assuming semantic guard implication. It links directly to DA16's degree-hard family and BW16's physical transport.

\begin{lemma}[Weighted Boolean guard DAG discharge]\label{lem:bd16-weighted}
In the common original squarefree Boolean polynomial ring over $\mathbb F_2$, let $P$ be the authenticated source axioms. A finite acyclic graph has node predicate indicators $g_v$ of degree at most $r$, with root indicator $1$. Every leaf has a BPC refutation from $P$ and $1+g_v$ of degree at most $D$, including source provenance. Each internal node supplies explicit coefficient polynomials $a_{ve}$ of degree at most $k$, and a BPC derivation from $P$ of an error polynomial $E_v$ of degree at most $e$ (meaning the entire derivation obeys this cap), such that the verified ring identity
\[
g_v=E_v+\sum_{e:v\to u}a_{ve}g_u
\]
holds. Then $P$ has a BPC refutation of degree at most $\max(D+r,r+k,e)$, independent of graph size and unfolded path count. Guards and weights need not be affine, disjoint, or source-independent.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
For a leaf multiply every proof line by $g_v$. Source axiom products are derived by monomial multiplications and XOR; the added guard axiom times $g_v$ is zero since $g_v^2=g_v$. Multiplication by a variable and XOR commute with this map, and every reduced product of a leaf line has degree at most $D+r$. The resulting final line derives $g_v=0$. In reverse topological order, from each already derived $g_u=0$ derive $a_{ve}g_u=0$ by monomial multiplications and XOR, in degree at most $r+k$. Add the supplied source derivation of $E_v=0$ and use the exact polynomial identity to derive $g_v=0$. The root yields $1=0$. Shared lines are derived once, with each consumer charged. No source model is assumed and no implication is inferred solely from a guard label.
\end{proof}

For a two-way query $q$ with Boolean value, write $B_0=g_v(1+q)$ and $B_1=g_v q$. The branch indicators sum to $g_v$. Choose edge weights $B_b$ and $E_v=\sum_b B_b(1+g_{u_b})$. A certified source-dependent implication at each edge is exactly a derivation of the corresponding error polynomial as zero. This permits forgetting and strengthening under certified source conditions, rather than requiring DR16's source-independent weakening. For supplied candidate children and an explicitly acquired span of certified source consequences, bounded-degree weights are discoverable by linear solving for $g_v$ in the span of monomial multiples of child indicators and the error span, followed by exact identity verification. A failed search declines.

\paragraph{Consequence for the universal progress obligation.}
With fixed degree caps, full degree-bounded BPC saturation as in DA16 finds a contradiction whenever any such DAG exists, even without acquiring the DAG: iterate each variable-multiplication image intersected with the low-degree subspace until the span stops growing. The monomial universe and number of strict enlargements are polynomial for fixed caps; preserve preimages and proof provenance. Thus DA16's hard family excludes universal fixed-cap refutation by these nonlinear source-certified weighted DAGs, regardless of graph size, and BW16 transports the obstruction to the native physical chart under its affine proof-lift hypotheses. No bound is asserted for high-degree sparse or factored certificates, non-PC certificates, extensions without a bounded-degree source lift, arbitrary sharing architectures, or unrestricted SAT. Fixed-cap saturation is an UNSAT terminal, not a complete SAT decision procedure. U-PROGRESS still requires a uniform decision-only mechanism outside these restricted premises, or a polynomial-bit high-degree certificate with paid verification and discovery.

\begin{lemma}[Factored Boolean identity verification warning]\label{lem:bd16-identity}
Testing whether an explicitly given Boolean polynomial circuit vanishes on every Boolean input is coNP-complete, even when the circuit is a product of complements of rank-at-most-three source clause violation polynomials. A source-independent guard weakening test from $1$ to the complement of that circuit is equally hard.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
For a 3-CNF with violation polynomials $P_i$, the linear-size factored circuit $C=\prod_i(1+P_i)$ is its satisfaction indicator on the Boolean cube. It is zero everywhere exactly when the formula is UNSAT; a nonzero evaluation is a polynomially checkable witness to failure. The guard inclusion from the whole cube into $\{1+C=1\}$ is the same decision. This is Boolean functional identity, not ordinary formal polynomial identity testing.
\end{proof}
A high-degree factored DAG therefore needs an independent deterministic verification mechanism, not a free semantic identity check. Circuit proof complexity and formal PIT are relevant but distinct; see Grochow--Pitassi, \href{https://arxiv.org/abs/1404.3820}{arXiv:1404.3820}, and Grochow, \href{https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.02184}{arXiv:2306.02184}. For fixed caps, dense coefficient arithmetic and explicit graph/trace verification are polynomial in input and supplied graph/trace length. All source acquisition, failed coefficient searches, canonicalization, local closure, exact guard provenance, DAG consumers, verification and storage are charged. On the same serial realization $CM\le(T+1)S$; a bound polynomial in original $N$ on a growing-cap certificate and its acquisition is unproved.

\paragraph{Executed common-residual controls.}
Fano 012,034,056,135,146,236,245 and the M22 plane on coordinates $3i+j$, identity source maps, empty guard and actual $x_0$ pins. Construct a five-node DAG with root query $x_2$, then $q=x_0x_1$ at both parent nodes. Shared child indicators $C_b=(q\text{ or }1+q)(1+P_0)$ deliberately strengthen the guards using an actual source owner. Each branch $B$ satisfies $B(1+C_b)=BP_0$; two edges have nonzero error on each root, checked in the degree-six Macaulay span. Plane both leaves refute in degree-four Macaulay span, Fano one leaf only; no false root on 21 sign neighbors (ten models each) and 14 budget neighbors (five models each). Macaulay is not full BPC. A separate full image-intersection saturation reproduces DA16/M22 joint closure: six Fano/plane root and actual $x_0$ pin charts stall at degree two and refute at degree three; all six individual plane side supports have 102/51 models (root/pin) and decline at degree three. Thus joint source algebra bypasses the marginal fixed point, and the nonlinear DAG does not improve the direct fixed-degree bound here. First checker 1864 assertions, 54219 products, 259 tiny exhaustive singleton affine-hull scans, no strict changes; second checker 272 assertions, 18913 image products, 52 rounds. No full proof trace replay, native lift replay, BS15 ring, large degree-hard family, full cooperative solver, formal kernel or measured physical CM executed. See \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/8a7274ad8c72b83074f27809ed96590a6fb3965e}{report}, \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/70b5098f6a75cefccad980cba58558b3b649b6aa}{checker}, \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/01a01b6f6e3689c905b1d83e2f688ba03f267273}{results}, \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/ec17725f3e029ea1c65c8ca7d137d1ce708930eb}{joint checker}, and \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/e6aa8b8cbd8fa4199c8b069f884a4de9453c07f6}{joint results}.

\paragraph{Joint stagnation protocol.}
On the same Fano and plane sources with identity maps, actual $x_0$ pins and nonlinear guards above, request complementary contributions from Algebra/Hub/MS (high-degree polynomial-bit identities or non-PC decisions), Matching/Interfaces (nonlinear joint occupancy and circulation), Wheels (native authenticated lifts), GD (factored nonlinear sharing with paid verifier), Potential/Obstruction/Red Team (audit weighted identities and aggregate consumers), Solver/Synthesis (combine accepted deductions and repeat certified closure). Next use BS15 rings with their existing source map and seed, and separately DA16's hard family, without claiming coincidence. Preserve conditional guards, compare individual and combined progress, and charge every unsuccessful search. No peer contribution beyond cited results is claimed executed. U-PROGRESS and unconditional COST remain OPEN. No public release.


\section{Relative-observation width survives exact affine closure (I22)}\label{sec:i22}
This attacks U-PROGRESS and the S21 positive-promise acquisition premise, not just an isolated terminal. GD22's supplied seam decomposition solves the BS15 ring despite exponential flat cut coverage; M22's combined side filters stagnate on the genuine four-partition source. We test whether even exact affine closure and gauge cancellation make a favorable relative-observation decomposition universally available.

\begin{lemma}[Basis-invariant raw owner width obstruction]\label{lem:i22-width}
There exist arbitrarily large positive linear NAE-3SAT sources on $n=3k$ variables with four supplied perfect clause partitions, whose exact satisfying support has full affine hull, but every subcubic decomposition of their original owner registry has shared relative-observation width $\Omega(n)$. The same width at every cut is preserved under either value of one actual seed coordinate. More generally, restricting to an actual consistent affine guard of rank $r$ can reduce each original cut width by at most $r$, including after arbitrary invertible chart changes. This concerns the original owner registry, not arbitrary equivalent nonlinear rewrites or solved-owner elimination.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
Take a simple connected 4-regular bipartite edge expander $G$ with sides $A,B$ of size $k\ge12$, and a fixed positive edge expansion constant $h$. Infinitely many such graphs exist, e.g. by the regular bipartite case of Brito--Dumitriu--Harris, \href{https://arxiv.org/abs/1804.07808}{arXiv:1804.07808}. Decompose the edges into four perfect matchings by repeated bipartite matching. Add a third class $C$ of size $k$. For matching $j$, assign its edges bijectively to $C$ avoiding any previously used $A$--$C$ or $B$--$C$ pair. Each row and column of the assignment forbids at most $2(j-1)\le6$ entries. The remaining balanced bipartite graph has minimum degree at least $k/2$ and satisfies Hall: sets of at most $k/2$ left vertices have enough neighbors and larger sets meet every right neighborhood. Hence polynomial augmenting-path search gives the four triple partitions. The source is linear, positive and genuinely four-partitioned.

Each class-indicator word is a model. For each vertex $v$ choose an indicator of a different class; both it and its flip at $v$ are models (in each incident triple $v$ was a majority bit). Their differences include every unit vector, certifying full joint affine hull by explicit polynomially many verified witnesses. Normalizing all these words by global complement to actual seed $x_s=b$ applies an affine map with linear part $x\mapsto x+x_s{\bf1}$, whose image is the entire seed direction hyperplane. Thus the guarded joint hull also fills its ambient chart. No additional affine deduction exists in either case.

Use relative row space $\operatorname{span}(e_a+e_b,e_a+e_c)$ at each owner $(a,b,c)\in A\times B\times C$; the forbidden relative word is zero. Write $U_L,U_R$ for sums of owner row spaces across a cut and $w=\dim(U_L\cap U_R)$. Expand each owner leaf into a two-row fork, retaining original cuts and introducing only cuts of width at most one. Delete the $e_a+e_c$ leaves and suppress degree-two nodes. Intersection dimension cannot increase on deletion; surviving rows represent the cycle matroid of $G$. The conventional matroid branchwidth counts intersection dimension plus one. By Mazoit--Thomasse, \href{https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/abs/surveys-in-combinatorics-2007/bandwidth-of-graphic-matroids/C9CA233149A3C3A818092BF277B25614}{Branchwidth of graphic matroids}, this equals graph branchwidth for bridgeless graphs; $G$ is bridgeless by even degrees.

Any subcubic edge-leaf tree of $G$ has a cut with each side containing at least $4k/3$ edges (leaf-weighted centroid). Let $B_0$ be the shared endpoints; each pure-side endpoint set has size at least $2k/3-|B_0|$ by degree four. The smaller pure set has at most $k$ vertices and all its external edges enter $B_0$. Expansion gives $h(2k/3-|B_0|)\le4|B_0|$, or $|B_0|\ge2hk/[3(4+h)]$. Hence the graph branchwidth, and therefore the maximum relative width of every original owner tree, is linear in $k$.

For an actual affine guard with row space $H$ of rank $r$, restriction to its free chart is the quotient map $\pi$ of dual rows by $H$. Since $\pi(U_L\cap U_R)\subseteq\pi(U_L)\cap\pi(U_R)$, we have $w_{\rm guard}\ge w-r$. Invertible basis changes preserve dimensions. In particular every adaptive raw-owner tree with logarithmic width needs total guard/equation rank $\Omega(n)$ on this family. For $H=\operatorname{span}(e_s)$ all original relative rows have even weight, so $\pi$ is injective on their total span and all intersections are preserved exactly. This proves seed-pin invariance. It does not imply any lower bound on the number of branches or on decision time.
\end{proof}

\paragraph{Joint stagnation and constructive bypass.}
On the exact BS15 ring, I22 verifies actual original feasible pair $x,y$ and common word $z$ and its complement, with the source dictionary $3i+j$, same exception and seed as M22/GD22. Every proper block-prefix owner cut (assign by minimum source coordinate) has relative width 5 in the four tested sizes; this complements GD22's supplied eight-state seam DP, not a new automatic decomposition search. On the M22 affine plane all six side supports have 102 words, but the joint positive NAE triangle-cut objective is at most $\lfloor9^2/4\rfloor=20<24$ required: every pair occurs once and each legal owner cuts two edges. Fano likewise has at most 12 cut edges versus 14 required. These elementary integer certificates survive either seed pin and agree with DA16's joint degree-three deductions; marginal closure alone cannot see them. On the new high-width SAT family, a supplied class indicator is already an actual polynomially verifiable witness, so raw width is not hardness. Finding an arbitrary rainbow class dictionary is not presumed easy or required by the theorem.

Executed I22: 14,646 assertions, seed 123456789; three explicit tripartite samples ($n=36,72,144$) with witnessed joint hull ranks $n$ and $n-1$ under seed, 80 sampled balanced cuts per size with relative ranges 26--35, 53--66, 111--131, exact seed invariance and general rank-drop tests. Samples are not certified expanders and sampled cuts are not minimum widths. BS15 $q=1,2,4,8$ with verified source partitions and both seed witnesses, 11 prefix widths; exhaustive plane/Fano joint labels and maximum cuts, all six plane side counts. Local coordinate closure to fixed point at root and both actual pins gives no extra deduction; global affine fullness for tripartite samples is certified by actual witnesses. No generic repair, native replay, full flow backend, minimum width search, formal kernel or physical CM measured. See \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/2ae8773ddae8d875b624a67223187b7e674ad82a}{report}, \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/fdc3eb86863e825127cbc875786087b754dafc93}{checker}, \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/a9d87cd0cac7f75aa51e0ffee29469812c9d39b0}{results}.

All adapter, witness construction and verification, row/guard acquisition, rank scans and retention are polynomial in explicit source and supplied plan; $CM\le(T+1)S$ on the same serial realization. Exponential interface upper bounds are not lower bounds on actual reachable states. No universal width guarantee follows even from full affine closure; no separation follows. Request complementary existing peers on these identical authenticated residuals and actual guards: algebra/Hub/MS nonlinear high-width deductions and integer cut certificates; Matching/BUILD Solver one verified common repair and actual flow lift; Wheels I21 native transport and alternate geometry with certified source map; GD/MS DAG factored exact seam and nonlinear summaries; Obstruction/Potential/Red Team/Synthesis aggregate reserve and hostile controls. Reclose after every accepted deduction, retain guards, exchange exact witnesses and provenance and charge declined work. The I22 report contains the bounded literature/official-repository search and limitations. U-PROGRESS and unconditional COST remain OPEN. No public release.


\section{One-mismatch acquisition on genuine four-partition sources (DS16)}\label{sec:ds16}
S21 (Lemmas~\ref{lem:s21-cycle} and \ref{lem:s21-hard}) leaves a gap between one-mismatch hardness in general synchronized circulations and the sufficient up-to-$L$ repair routine on the genuine four-partition geometry. BS15 and GD22 exhibit large moves and a factored bypass, while M22 and DA16 show respectively stalled marginal filters and a successful joint degree-three plane refutation. The following adaptive reduction closes the single-mismatch \emph{search} quantifier gap, not U-PROGRESS.

\begin{lemma}[Transparent completion of partial partitions]\label{lem:ds16-complete}
A positive linear ternary NAE registry on $n\equiv0\pmod3$ vertices with four supplied partial partitions has a polynomially constructible extension to four perfect partitions, on at most $57n$ vertices. Every original word extends by the same fixed auxiliary word, satisfying all added clauses, and every completed model restricts to a model of the partial registry.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
For each color $p$ group vertices missing that color into triples, possible because their count is $n-3m_p$. For each such triple take two fresh copies of the 21-vertex template with vertices $A_k,B_k,C_k$ for $k\in\mathbb Z_7$, and color-$q$ clauses $(A_k,B_{k+q},C_{k+2q})$, $q=0,1,2,3$. Each color is perfect and linearity follows from the distinct differences $q,2q$ modulo seven between the respective groups. In copy zero set $A=0,B=1,C=0$ and in copy one take the complement. Delete each copy's color-$p$ clause indexed by $k=0$; pair the three corresponding vertices across the copies and attach one missing original vertex to each pair in a new color-$p$ clause. The two filler bits are opposite, so the attached original bit is unrestricted. These cross-copy pairs and original/filler pairs are new, and all remaining clauses keep their old incidences. This proves exact preservation, perfectness and linearity. At most $4n/3$ missing triples require 42 vertices each. Discovery, explicit construction, checks and lifting are polynomial bit operations.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[Exact slack creation in every component]\label{lem:ds16-slack}
Any positive linear NAE instance with four perfect partitions has a polynomial-size equisatisfiable partial-partition registry, containing all original variables and preserving their entire model projection, in which every nonempty clause-intersection component contains a vertex missing a color. The number of vertices remains a multiple of three.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
Use the actual nine-vertex M22 affine plane on IDs $3i+j$, colors $a=0,1,2$ for the triples $j=ai+b\bmod3$, and color 3 for fixed $i$. Every pair is covered once. If $k$ vertices have value one, the number of monochromatic pairs is $\binom{k}{2}+\binom{9-k}{2}\ge16$. Each legal clause contributes one such pair and each bad clause three, so at least $(16-12)/2=2$ clauses are violated. Remove the two color-zero lines $(0,3,6)$ and $(1,4,7)$, obtaining ten-clause gadget $G$. Every model of $G$ makes both omitted lines monochromatic; in particular terminals $1,4,7$ are equal. The explicit word $010010011$ (in ID order) and its complement satisfy $G$. The color-three rows and color-one transversals show that $G$ is connected. Vertices $0,3,6$ miss color zero.

In each nonempty component of the source replace one color-$p$ clause $(a,b,c)$ as follows. Allocate a private $G$ for each original terminal $t\in\{a,b,c\}$, relabel colors by addition of $p$ modulo four, insert color-$p$ clause $(t,1_t,4_t)$ and then insert color-$p$ clause $(7_a,7_b,7_c)$. Each original terminal is opposite its three equal private terminals, so the last clause enforces exactly the original NAE constraint. Every legal old word lifts using the two explicit $G$ words. The converse follows from equality and the last clause. Each original terminal uses its one vacated color slot; pair $(1_t,4_t)$ was in the removed line and nowhere else; all other added pairs are fresh. Partial partitions and linearity survive. Each component stays connected and includes the spare color at the private vertex $0_t$. There are 27 new variables and a net increase of 33 owners per replaced clause. All maps and lifts are explicit.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Single-mismatch search equivalence on the genuine S21 geometry]\label{thm:ds16-one}
Suppose a fixed deterministic procedure, uniformly polynomially capped in the explicit input size, takes a genuine positive linear four-perfect-partition NAE instance with a supplied pairing of colors and two individually feasible S21 circulation witnesses differing on exactly one actual variable arc. Whenever a common word exists, the procedure returns a valid common word (or its two flow witnesses). On negative instances it may decline or time out; invalid results are never accepted. Then $\mathrm P=\mathrm{NP}$. Conversely $\mathrm P=\mathrm{NP}$ implies the stated search guarantee.
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}
The genuine starting class is NP-complete by Darmann--D\"ocker--Dorn, \href{https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03395}{arXiv:2412.03395}, Section 3.2. Apply Lemma~\ref{lem:ds16-slack}. In each clause-intersection component choose a root clause incident to a vertex missing a permanent color and order clauses in reverse breadth-first spanning-tree order. For every newly activated clause $e$ of color $p$, some vertex $v\in e$ still misses a color $q\ne p$ after activation: a nonroot shares a vertex with its later parent, whose color differs from $p$, and the root has its permanent spare. Process prefixes monotonically, starting with an arbitrary word on the empty registry. Maintain a satisfying word on the previous prefix.

If the new clause is legal, retain the word. Otherwise only $e$ is bad in the new prefix.
