\section{DA19: Exact neighborhood-code quotient and nonlinear residual rank}\label{sec:da19}
The target remains Obligation~\ref{obl:progress}. S24's actual tuple fusion may grow exponentially and DO19's factor recognition declines on the same five connected pair charts. RT24's component equations are necessary but not sufficient. Instead factor exact exterior behavior into shared labels without merging owner tuples. All actual guards must already be encoded in the explicit binary selector registry.
\begin{lemma}[Exact neighborhood-code quotient and paid projected enumeration]\label{lem:da19-code}
After certified arc closure, partition the rows of each owner $i$ for each other owner $j$ by equality of live neighborhoods in $j$. Compatibility is constant on every product of two classes. If the reduced bipartite class graph is a matching covering both sides (the original relation is a union of disjoint complete bipartite components), use common binary class-label bits for this pair, numbering matched classes identically. Complete relations need no bits. Otherwise use independent binary labels on each side and retain the explicit table of allowed class pairs. For each owner store the distinct concatenated incident class-label words and one actual row representative per word. The resulting owner and pair factors are exactly decision-equivalent to the original registry, with polynomial acquisition and reconstruction in explicit chart length.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
Every original transversal induces a compatible label word. Conversely each owner table supplies an actual row, and each shared label or class-pair factor guarantees compatibility for every representative in the indicated classes by the definition of neighborhood equality. Thus an actual transversal reconstructs, followed by earlier source lifts. This is exact on the projection to label words, not necessarily on internal representative values; DP19's guard-transport firewall applies. New restrictions on removed representatives require a separate proof.

For each deduplicated factor $T$ compute its affine hull from differences to an anchor. It is exact iff $|T|=2^{\dim\operatorname{aff}T}$. Collect all hull equations into $Ax=b$; inconsistency rejects. Let $J$ be the union of scopes of the factors that are not equal to their hulls. For a consistent system the residual observation rank is
\[
 r=\dim\pi_J(\ker A)=|J|-\operatorname{rank}A+\operatorname{rank} A_{\bar J}.
\]
Indeed the kernel of that projection is the nullspace of $A_{\bar J}$. Find a projected kernel basis with stored full preimages by Gaussian elimination. Enumerate the $2^r$ projected words of the affine solution set, one full lift per word, testing the nonaffine tables exactly. The affine factors hold automatically; the nonaffine tests depend only on $J$. A passing word lifts by the first part; exhausting the projected support rejects. Independently, a factor row outside the projection of $Ax=b$ onto its scope cannot occur in a legal solution and may be deleted with a Gaussian certificate and original class map. Reclose after accepted deletions and at stagnation; row/edge removals must be transported to actual row IDs before changing the active representation.

For $m$ owners and $R$ original live rows the number of label bits is $O(m^2\log(R+1))$, total owner rows at most $R$ and pair table entries at most $R^2$. Dense acquisition, deduplication, hulls, rank and projection checks are polynomial in explicit chart length $L$. The decision cost is $2^r\operatorname{poly}(L)$, hence polynomial in original $N$ only on a fixed polynomial chart cap and a fixed logarithmic bound on $r$. All discovery, unsuccessful checks, prior acquisition, guard and source maps, and witness verification are paid. On this admitted serial execution peak space is polynomial and $\mathrm{CM}\le(T+1)S$. Use the representation as scratch unless an independent aggregate credit check permits committing it: no reset of DR18 or S24 reserves. Strict certified deletions in the original row/edge registry pay repetitions; no universal strict deletion or logarithmic rank bound follows.
\end{proof}
\begin{lemma}[Label-independent nonaffinity and raw-rank obstruction]\label{lem:da19-barrier}
A nonrectangular binary relation cannot become affine under separate injective encodings of its two sides. Every raw unpinned signed linear 3-uniform NAE registry of minimum variable occurrence two has only rectangular pair relations but the minimal binary neighborhood-code construction has residual rank $r=n$ on its $n$ occurring variables.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}
Affine relations are closed under ternary XOR; three allowed corners of a rectangle imply the fourth. This implication is invariant under separate injective encodings. In the raw NAE chart each nontrivial pair shares precisely one coordinate and its class label is that bit, possibly complemented. Each owner observes all its three bits through its incident classes, so its code has six distinct words, not a power of two, and is nonaffine even under other injective encodings. The six raw NAE words have full affine hull on the three bits; the hull of the repeated binary labels thus imposes precisely agreement (with possible complements) between their copies. Global hull equations identify exactly all labels observing the same actual variable, since the owners observing a variable form a clique of pair links. All variables remain free in that hull, and every label is in a nonlinear owner scope. Thus $r=n$. Growing registries defeat a universal logarithmic residual-rank theorem for this adapter, irrespective of all pairs being rectangular; other representations, nonlinear deductions, factored algorithms and unrestricted decision are not excluded. Rectangularity and affine tractability are standard in CSP theory; compare Bulatov--Dalmau, \href{https://doi.org/10.1137/050628957}{A Simple Algorithm for Mal'tsev Constraints} (2006). Pair rectangularity alone supplies no common polymorphism for all owner tables.
\end{proof}
\paragraph{Executed same-chart comparison.}
Read the five immutable BD18 pair charts and MS24 combined and BH18 mode-true pair deltas via S24's exact input manifest. Verify live symmetry and delta subset, AC initially and after each imported batch. All ten jobs remain OPEN with no additional impossible code row under global hull projection. Residual ranks baseline versus combined: DR17 590/594, BW17 255/255, M23 root 810/810, actual pin-zero 766/766, actual pin-one 769/769. DR17 label bits increase 1148 to 1165, rectangular edges 62 to 60, nonrectangular 161 to 165. No owner-row compression or affine owner factor on these shared charts; nonrectangular pair factors are nonaffine. Hence sound edge deletion can raise this numerical rank; do not use it as a universally decreasing potential. Exact projected enumeration capped at 12 in the checker. Fano UNSAT and single-sign SAT neighbor independently have 0 and 10 source models, both residual rank seven, and enumeration agrees (128 and 8 words visited). Duplicated parity cycles on 5/11/31 owners: six raw rows per owner, two class words; even SAT with a verified representative transversal, odd UNSAT by inconsistent affine hull, rank zero nonlinear support. AC fixed on these synthetic controls by structure. No additional source witness or lift replay on the shared charts, no post-S24 fused-chart run, no separate BH18 ternary-nogood consumption, upstream proof replay, full portfolio, native replay, formal kernel, LaTeX compilation or measured physical CM. \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/deb30f2ef72da05a94c6ba0c6ec8fe6a5b70ed45}{Report}; \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/2a2e527859e273c8fe32b522ed40bd2fbc9afc57}{checker}; \href{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/commit/fdced321f8d1f8c231f864a2feefe210a72d2b62}{results and immutable input references}.
